> - 影響: Customers on the zscloud.net cloud intermittently experienced Admin Portal unavailability, API errors, and an inability to save or activate policy and configuration. SCIM provisioning was also affected. Further assessment confirmed that new device registrations and Zscaler Client Connector enrollments were not affected. Existing user connections, traffic forwarding, and security policy enforcement remained unaffected throughout the incident.
> Zscaler has detected and mitigated the issue affecting administrative services on the zscloud.net cloud and is monitoring service stability. During the incident, customers may have experienced intermittent Admin Portal login failures, API errors, and an inability to save or activate policy and configuration changes. > Products &amp; Solutions | Zscaler This site uses JavaScript to provide a number of functions, to use this site please enable JavaScript in your browser. Request a demo Contact Us Show Sign In Zscaler Cloud Portal Zscaler Cloud Portal One Zscaler Cloud Portal Two Zscaler Cloud Portal Three Zscaler Cloud Portal Beta admin.zscloud.net Zscaler Private Access Cloud Portal One Zscaler Private Access Cloud Portal Two Show English Français Deutsch Italiano 日本語 Castellano - Mexico Castellano - España Portugues - Brasil Home Platform Solutions Resources Company Zscaler&#x27;s Unified Platform The Zscaler unified cybersecurity platform, built on the Zero Trust Exchange. One platform makes your attack surface disappear and scales with AI. Learn More Zero Trust SASE Everywhere Security for AI Data Security Agentic SecOps Zero Trust SASE Everywhere Secure users, devices, workloads, and agents with the only SASE built for zero trust and AI. Learn More Secure Internet Access (ZIA) Protect users when they browse the internet or use SaaS apps. Secure Private Access (ZPA) Connect users to private apps without a VPN. Zero Trust Branch Connect users and devices across branches, campuses, and factories. Zero Trust Cloud Transform your workload security from firewalls to zero trust. Digital Experience (ZDX) Rapidly detect and resolve app, network, and device issues. Zero Trust Browser Secure browsing and private app access on any device. Cloud Sandbox Defend against unknown file-based threats. Endpoint Context (New) Extend and fine-tune inline policy with process-level context Zero Trust Firewall Extend traffic inspection to all ports and protocols. > Agents on the Windows operating system exclude infrastructure traffic (i.e., loopback IP addresses) from the flow logs, reducing noise and improving visibility. With this update, both Windows and Linux agents provide this streamlined flow data for organizations to monitor and analyze.

> New cloud applications are added to the cloud application categories. You can download the list of newly added cloud applications to the respective categories: [Download](/sites/default/files/downloads/zia/documentation-knowledgebase/policies/cloud-apps/cloud-app-control-policies/cloud-app-categories/New_Cloud_Apps_April-2026.xlsx)
> The risk index and risk attribute values for these new applications are visible only after they are available on all the clouds. Refer to the [trust post](https://trust.zscaler.com/zscalerthree.net/notification/452301) for details.
> Logs for Zscaler 3rd-Party App Governance and Posture Management are supported by Zscaler's Nanolog Streaming Service (NSS). You can configure NSS and Cloud NSS feeds for both log types to stream their respective data to your security information and event management (SIEM) system, providing a unified view of your organization’s security posture for effective threat management.
> Access to these log types requires a subscription to [Advanced SaaS Security Posture Management (SSPM)](/zia/what-advanced-posture-management). To subscribe to Advanced SSPM, contact your Zscaler Account team.
> [See image.](#img-app-governance-posture-log-types)
